The gain persists after rendering and OCR. Passive scores from the same Qwen3-4B layer and direct prompting of a same-scale instruction model perform substantially worse on flattened records. Across six language models, RR-selected cuts also undergo consistently less local output disruption than unselected candidate edges. An observed-token likelihood-ratio readout is competitive in some architectures, indicating that the central contribution is the intervention: context dependence itself can provide a boundary signal when surface structure is weak.

</details>


### [131] [Equitable System-Prompt Selection via Constrained Mixed-Strategy GroupDRO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04339)
*Mengyu Xu,Qiaoxin Yang,Zhihan Liu,Ruiyao Xu,Zachary Liu,Kezhen Chen,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于约束混合策略GroupDRO的系统提示选择框架，通过为现有提示池分配权重来最小化最差情况下的信息质量损失，同时保持平均质量接近平均选择。在五个LLM的双语医疗和消费者金融基准上，该方法将整体均值、最差25%均值和最差值分别降低了13.1%、13.2%和13.7%，验证了多提示互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在信息检索中，语义等价的问句因措辞不同可能得到质量差异很大的回答。系统提示通常针对平均情况优化，导致某些问句措辞获得不完整或低质量的回答。因此需要一种方法来最小化最差情况下的信息质量损失。

Method: 构建一个约束混合策略的GroupDRO框架，不对提示文本进行优化，而是为现有系统提示池中的每个提示分配权重，以最小化跨评估指标和组（不同问句措辞）的最差情况信息质量损失，同时约束平均损失使其接近基于平均选择的损失。提示池生成和权重选择解耦，适用于任何提示池，并可利用互补提示的集成。

Result: 在五个LLM上，使用双语医疗和消费者金融两个基准测试，与无缓解措施相比，约束方法平均降低了整体均值13.1%、最差25%均值13.2%和最差值13.7%，同时整体质量接近平均选择。多提示权重揭示了指标-组对之间的互补性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了不同问句措辞导致的信息质量差异，通过集成互补系统提示提升了最差情况下的响应质量，且不显著牺牲平均性能。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for information seeking, yet semantically equivalent questions phrased in different ways can receive answers of considerably different quality. System prompts are widely employed to steer response behavior, but they are typically optimized for average-case quality, so some question phrasings may still receive incomplete or low-quality answers. To address this, we formulate a constrained mixed-strategy GroupDRO framework for system-prompt selection. Instead of optimizing the system-prompt text, the framework assigns weights to system prompts in an existing pool to minimize the worst-case information-quality loss across evaluation metrics and groups, while constraining the mean loss to stay close to that of average-based selection. Because pool generation and selection are decoupled, the method applies to any system-prompt pool and can leverage an ensemble of complementary system prompts rather than a single one. Across five LLMs on two bilingual medical and consumer-finance benchmarks, the constrained method reduces the Overall Mean, Worst 25% Mean, and Worst by 13.1%, 13.2%, and 13.7% on average relative to no mitigation while keeping overall quality close to Average selection. Its multi-prompt weights reveal complementarity across metric-group pairs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Rainxu09/equitable-system-prompt-selection.

</details>


### [132] [The Calibration Floor: Format Repair Can Masquerade as Self-Correction at Small-to-Mid Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04355)
*Mingguang Chen,Bo Qu,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Accuracy changes after language-model self-revision are often interpreted as reasoning changes, but this paper shows they can fail at the answer-extraction boundary. Using multiple models (Qwen3.5, Gemma-4-12B, Tencent Hy3, Nvidia Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B), they decompose accuracy shifts into content margin (both answers parseable) and format-recovery/loss margins (parseability changes). Format effects dominate content effects. They causally test this via grammar-constrained decoding, closing 71% of the gap. Floor-scale models have higher content-level change odds. Frontier models show format-dominance intensifying with scale. Content is a minority share of measured self-correction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The field typically interprets accuracy changes after language-model self-revision as changes in reasoning. However, this interpretation can fail at the answer-extraction boundary due to changes in parseability (format effects). The authors aim to demonstrate this failure causally rather than only observationally, and to quantify how much of the measured self-correction effect is actually due to content (reasoning) versus format (answer extraction).

Method: The authors decompose the always-revise accuracy shift into a content margin (both answers parseable) and format-recovery/loss margins (changes in parseability). They test across 29 primary cells plus a frontier arm using models: Qwen3.5 (0.8B-9B), Gemma-4-12B, and two frontier models via API (Tencent Hy3, Nvidia Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B). To causally test, they force already-generated reasoning through grammar-constrained decoding so every answer is parseable by construction. They also replicate a cited confidence-gating protocol on Qwen3.5. A clustered model is used to compare floor-scale vs capable-scale models.

Result: On 12 cells with meaningful unparseable-answer rates, format effects exceed content effects (Wilcoxon p=1.7e-3). Grammar-constrained decoding closes a median 71% of the gap between the naive total effect and the content-margin estimate, with two cells converging exactly. Floor-scale (0.8B/2B) models have far higher odds of content-level change and harm than capable-scale models (p<1e-7). Replicating a confidence-gating protocol does not reproduce its reported gain and shows near-zero content margin. Frontier check on much larger models shows format-dominance intensifying with scale: content margin is exactly zero in all 5 cells despite total effects up to +0.275. Only one cell is marginally viable with negligible sealed-holdout gain.

Conclusion: Content is a minority share of what the field has measured as self-correction. The answer-extraction boundary (format effects) dominates accuracy shifts in language-model self-revision, challenging the common interpretation that these changes reflect genuine reasoning improvements. The authors release the instrument, code, and derived results.

Abstract: Accuracy changes after language-model self-revision are usually interpreted as changes in reasoning. We show this can fail at the answer-extraction boundary, and test the failure causally rather than only observationally. Across Qwen3.5 (0.8B-9B), Gemma-4-12B, and two frontier models via API (Tencent Hy3, Nvidia Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B) in 29 primary cells plus a frontier arm, we decompose the always-revise accuracy shift into a content margin (both answers parseable) and format-recovery/loss margins (parseability changes). On 12 cells with meaningful unparseable-answer rates, format effects exceed content effects (Wilcoxon p=1.7e-3). To test this causally, we force already-generated reasoning through grammar-constrained decoding so every answer is parseable by construction: across 14 cells this closes a median 71% of the gap between the naive total effect and the content-margin estimate, with two cells converging exactly and a residual on the two largest-effect cells reported rather than dismissed. A clustered model confirms floor-scale (0.8B/2B) models have far higher odds of content-level change and harm than capable-scale models (p<1e-7). Replicating a cited confidence-gating protocol verbatim on Qwen3.5 does not reproduce its reported gain and shows the same near-zero content margin. A frontier check on much larger models shows format-dominance intensifying with scale: content margin is exactly zero in all 5 cells despite total effects up to +0.275, though this arm is lower-powered. The calibration-floor criterion on the content margin reveals a squeeze: floor-scale cells have headroom but insufficient signal, capable-scale cells have signal but little headroom; only one cell is marginally viable, with negligible sealed-holdout gain. Content is a minority share of what the field has measured as self-correction. We release the instrument, code, and derived results.

</details>


### [133] [FinReportBench: Measuring and Improving Institution-Grade Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04374)
*Yinghao Tang,Tan Zhenwei,Yiyao Wang,Wanli Gu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FinReportBench 是一个面向机构级金融报告生成的专家基础基准，包含35项评估指标、244个双语任务，覆盖可交付性、报告身份和机构完整性。研究发现基础可交付性已接近饱和，但报告身份和机构完整性仍是主要瓶颈。通过基准引导的技能蒸馏，在五个模型系列上平均提升G1 33.85分、G2 13.83分，且不损害G0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成流畅的金融分析，但流畅性本身不足以确定报告是否适合机构交付。专家评审发现报告身份、机构组件、源学科和视觉交付方面存在反复出现的差距，因此需要建立一个专家基础的基准来衡量和改进机构级金融报告生成。

Method: 通过专家部分排序、多模态证据和决策边界审计，推导出包含35个项目的评估标准，涵盖可交付性、报告身份和机构完整性。从10,000条中英文金融研究源记录中，策划了244个双语任务，涵盖三个研究对象和两个输入层级。每个任务分离公共查询、重构的研究轨迹和隐藏的源包。使用三个独立的评判系列以接近天花板水平复现专家部分排序，然后使用基准引导的技能蒸馏将反复出现的失败转化为可重复使用的生成和自我审查约束。

Result: 在九个模型系列中，基本可交付性接近饱和，而报告身份和机构完整性仍然是主要瓶颈。最大的跨模型差距涉及生成追踪控制、信息密度和数据纪律，而非基本的报告框架。通过基准引导的技能蒸馏，在五个模型系列上，演化后的技能在配对的无技能运行中平均提升G1 33.85分，G2 13.83分，同时每对都保持G0不变。

Conclusion: FinReportBench 通过专家基础和结构化评估有效衡量机构级金融报告生成质量，且基准引导的技能蒸馏能显著提升模型性能。有界、可观察的标准支持可靠评估，代码和基准工件已公开。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent financial analysis, but fluency alone does not establish whether a report is suitable for institutional delivery. We introduce FinReportBench, an expert-grounded benchmark for measuring and improving institution-grade financial report generation. Expert review reveals recurring gaps in report identity, institutional components, source discipline, and visual delivery. We derive a 35-item rubric through expert partial orders, multimodal evidence, and audits of decision boundaries, covering deliverability, report identity, and institutional completeness. Starting from 10,000 balanced Chinese and English financial-research source records, we curate 244 bilingual tasks across three research objects and two input tiers. Each task separates the public query, reconstructed research trajectory, and hidden source packet. Three independent judge families reproduce the expert partial order at near-ceiling rates, showing that bounded, observable criteria support reliable evaluation. Across nine model families, basic deliverability is nearly saturated, while report identity and institutional completeness remain the primary bottlenecks. The largest cross-model gaps concern generation-trace control, information density, and data discipline rather than basic report framing. We then use benchmark-guided skill distillation to turn recurrent failures into reusable generation and self-review constraints. Across five model families, the evolved skill improves mean G1 by 33.85 points and mean G2 by 13.83 points over paired no-skill runs while preserving G0 for every pair. Code and benchmark artifacts are available at https://github.com/MisterBrookT/finreportbench.

</details>


### [134] [EdgeLM: Edge Demonstrations for Language Models' Table Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04390)
*Soroush Omidvartehrani,Mohammadamin Habibollah,Mohammadreza Daviran,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EdgeLM 是一种面向表格预测任务的上下文学习演示选择框架，它通过检索两类边缘证据（数据边缘和模型边缘）来代替传统的相似性检索，在多个任务和模型上持续取得最优或接近最优的性能，且无需模型重新训练或任务特定工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于相似性的检索方法倾向于选择与查询相似的演示，但这往往会强化模型已有的预测倾向，而非揭示决策边界附近的关键区分信息。对于难以决策的样本，仅靠相似性无法提供足够的信息来帮助模型做出正确判断。

Method: EdgeLM 提出检索两种互补的边缘证据：(1) 数据边缘（data edges），即与查询邻近但真实标签不同的样本；(2) 模型边缘（model edges），即与查询相似但当前模型先前分类错误的样本。该方法不依赖模型重新训练或任务特定的工程设计，可直接应用于任意 LLM。

Result: 在五个数据整理任务、十五个数据集以及五个开源和专有 LLM 上，EdgeLM 在每一设置下均达到最佳或接近最佳的性能。消融实验表明，数据边缘和模型边缘两种证据提供了互补的收益。

Conclusion: EdgeLM 是一种简单而有效的演示选择策略，通过提供决策边界附近的信息性样本，显著提升了 LLM 在表格预测任务中的上下文学习能力，超越了传统的相似性检索方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform table-centric prediction through in-context learning, making demonstration selection critical to performance. Existing retrieval methods prioritize similarity to the query, but similar demonstrations often reinforce the model's likely prediction rather than reveal the distinctions needed for difficult decisions. We propose EdgeLM, a retrieval framework that instead selects edge evidence, demonstrations that are both relevant to the query and informative about the decision boundary. EdgeLM retrieves two complementary forms of edge evidence by selecting data edges, nearby examples with different ground-truth labels, and model edges, similar examples previously misclassified by the deployed model. EdgeLM requires neither model retraining nor task-specific engineering. Across five data wrangling tasks, fifteen datasets, and five open-weight and proprietary LLMs, EdgeLM consistently achieves the best or near-best performance in every setting, while ablations show that the two forms of edge evidence provide complementary benefits. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/soroushomidvar/EdgeLM.

</details>


### [135] [NOLLI: A Difficulty-Calibrated Puzzle Benchmark for Diagnosing the English-Korean Performance Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04397)
*Dasol Choi,Joonyong Park,Daegon Yu,Soo Yong Kim,Youngsook Song,Seunghyeok Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NOLLI 是一个由程序生成的英韩双语谜题基准，包含15种谜题类型（25个任务，7,500个条目），通过行为校准难度，采用三级设计（直接翻译、韩文书写系统适配、韩国文化专属任务），用于诊断韩语性能差距。实验表明，12个模型在英韩匹配任务上准确率统计等价（±10 pp），但书写系统密集型任务（如韩文Cipher）表现差距显著，且结构大小并非难度可靠代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对韩语性能差距的系统诊断，特别是书写系统（韩文jamo）和文化特异性任务的影响。作者希望构建一个可再生、确定性评分、难度行为校准的基准，以准确评估语言模型在韩语上的表现短板。

Method: 设计15种谜题类型（25任务，7,500项），每个实例可种子再生且唯一解。难度通过行为校准（调整生成器使参考模型落入目标准确率区间）。三级结构：匹配直译、韩文jamo书写系统适配（子音节字母）、韩国文化/正字法特有任务。评估15个前沿、开源及韩国本土模型。

Result: 12个模型（准确率>3%）在英韩匹配任务上准确率统计等价（±10 pp）；书写系统密集型任务中，韩文Cipher落后英文最多68.7 pp，而相同jamo的Cryptarithmetic无系统惩罚；Jamo Composition准确率可预测韩文Cipher；韩国专属任务区分规则应用赤字与亲属关系赤字（后者12个模型均存在）；结构大小在15种谜题类型中的7种无法区分Easy/Hard难度。

Conclusion: 仅呈现语言本身对性能影响很小，但书写系统（特别是多步子音节执行）带来显著挑战；韩国文化/正字法任务揭示不同类型的知识赤字；结构大小不是经验难度的可靠指标，行为校准至关重要。

Abstract: We introduce NOLLI, a procedurally generated English-Korean puzzle benchmark designed to diagnose where Korean performance gaps arise. It comprises 15 puzzle types (25 tasks; 7,500 items), with every instance seed-regenerable, verified to have a unique solution, and scored deterministically. Rather than equating harder with bigger, we calibrate difficulty behaviorally, tuning each generator until a fixed reference model lands in target accuracy bands. Its three-level design combines matched direct translations, script adaptations over Hangul jamo (sub-syllabic letters), and Korean-only tasks grounded in Korean culture or orthography. We evaluate 15 frontier, open-weight, and Korean-developed models; among the 12 above a 3% overall-accuracy floor, matched English-Korean accuracy is statistically equivalent within a +/- 10 pp margin (TOST), suggesting little cost from presentation language alone. Writing-system-intensive tasks show sharper gaps: Korean Cipher falls behind English by up to 68.7 pp, whereas Cryptarithmetic over the same jamo shows no systematic penalty, and Jamo Composition accuracy predicts Korean Cipher accuracy. These contrasts are diagnostic rather than causal, consistent with difficulty in multi-step sub-syllabic execution. Korean-only tasks separate rule-application deficits, which vary in sign, from a Kinship deficit positive in all 12. Finally, a salient size measure fails to grow from Easy to Hard in 7 of 15 types, making structural size an unreliable proxy for empirical difficulty.

</details>


### [136] [Social Pressure Breaks Majority Voting in LLM Safety Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04415)
*Yibo Hu,Jiaming Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在安全检测面板中受到误导性社会线索（错误标签）影响的现象。通过两轮实验，发现共享的错误上下文会显著提高面板的误报率，且模型对“不安全”标签的跟随倾向远高于“安全”标签，揭示了安全面板的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管使用多个LLM组成面板进行投票可以提高安全检测的准确性，但如果所有模型在投票前都接触到相同的误导性上下文，这种优势可能会消失。本研究旨在探索这种风险，即共享社会线索对安全面板的潜在影响。

Method: 进行两轮对照实验：每个模型先独立判断内容，然后再次判断，但此时会看到六个模拟同伴的判断结果（错误标签或弃权）。最后通过多数投票整合最终判断。实验涉及6个开源LLM和6个数据集。

Result: 错误标签的同伴消息使平均误报率从56.5%升至87.5%，多数投票使面板误报率达100%。模型对“不安全”标签的跟随率约75%，而对“安全”标签仅约17%。面板的漏报率变化不大。

Conclusion: 研究表明，共享社会线索是安全面板的一个失败模式，可能导致误报率飙升。论文提供了一个简单的部署前诊断方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to detect unsafe content. A common approach is to combine judgments from a panel of models to correct individual mistakes, but this benefit may disappear when every model sees the same misleading context before voting. We study this risk in a controlled two-round experiment. Each model first judges an item alone, then judges it again after six simulated peers either assert the wrong label or abstain. We combine the final judgments by majority vote. Across six open-weight LLMs and six datasets, we find that the wrong-label peer message raises the average reviewer false-alarm rate from 56.5% under silent peers to 87.5%, and majority voting raises the panel false-alarm rate to 100%. Without an asserted label, the same panel outperforms its average member. The effect is strongly asymmetric: reviewers follow pushes toward "unsafe" far more than pushes toward "safe" (about 75% versus 17%), so the panel's false-alarm rate rises sharply while its harmful-miss rate changes little. The proprietary-model probe shows substantial variation across models. These results identify susceptibility to shared social cues as a failure mode of safety panels and provide a simple pre-deployment diagnostic.

</details>


### [137] [MERaLiON-GR: Speech Gender Recognition Model for English and SEA Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04433)
*Qiongqiong Wang,Ai Ti Aw,Nancy F. Chen,Ying Lay Chiu,Yang Ding,Yingxu He,Ridong Jiang,Zhuohan Liu,Yanfeng Lu,Yi Ma,Muhammad Huzaifah,Nabilah Binte Md Johan,Nattadaporn Lertcheva,Pham Minh Duc,Sailor Hardik Bhupendra,Siti Umairah Binte Mohammad Salleh,Shuo Sun,Tarun Kumar Vangani,Jeremy H. M. Wong,Jinyang Wu,Longyin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MERaLiON-GR is a binary gender recognition system for English and Southeast Asian languages, finetuning a conformer-based speech encoder with LoRA and adding an ECAPA-TDNN downstream network with attention pooling and a linear classifier. It outperforms state-of-the-art models like Vox-Profile and large Audio-LLMs across multiple languages in both full-utterance and segment-level evaluations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To achieve accurate gender recognition (female/male) for English and Southeast Asian languages, surpassing existing state-of-the-art models and demonstrating the value of dedicated speech models for paralinguistic understanding and cross-lingual generalization.

Method: The system finetunes MERaLiON-SpeechEncoder-2, a large conformer-based transformer pre-trained on a broad speech corpus, using parameter-efficient fine-tuning via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). It appends a multi-scale ECAPA-TDNN downstream network with attention pooling and a lightweight linear classifier for binary gender classification.

Result: Extensive evaluations across multilingual Singaporean and Southeast Asian languages (English, Chinese, Malay, Tamil, Thai, Vietnamese, Indonesian, and Khmer) show that MERaLiON-GR consistently surpasses the state-of-the-art gender recognition model Vox-Profile and a large Audio-LLM in both full-utterance and segment-level evaluation modes.

Conclusion: The results underscore the value of dedicated speech models in achieving accurate paralinguistic understanding and strong cross-lingual generalization.

Abstract: We present MERaLiON-GR, a speech gender recognition system that performs binary classification (female / male) on English and Southeast Asian (SEA) languages. The model finetunes MERaLiON-SpeechEncoder-2, a large conformer based transformer pre-trained on a broad speech corpus, and applies parameter efficient fine-tuning via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to adapt the encoder to the gender recognition task, and appends a multi-scale ECAPA-TDNN down stream network with attention pooling and a lightweight linear classifier. Extensive evaluations across multilingual Singaporean and Southeast Asian languages (English, Chinese, Malay, Tamil, Thai, Vietnamese, Indonesian, and Khmer) show that MERaLiON-GR consistently surpasses the state-of-the-art gender recognition model Vox-Profile and a large Audio-LLM, in both full-utterance and segment level evaluation modes. The results underscore the value of dedicated speech models in achieving accurate paralinguistic understanding and strong cross-lingual generalization.

</details>


### [138] [D$^2$F-ReAG: Dynamic Decomposition and Filtering for Multi-Hop Reasoning-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04444)
*Jiaoyang Li,Junhao Ruan,Shengwei Tang,Kaiyan Chang,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: D2F-ReAG 提出了一种动态分解与过滤的多跳推理增强生成范式，通过判断根级推理的可靠性来决定直接回答或分解为子问题，从而提升多跳问答的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 依赖静态内部知识常生成不准确答案；RAG 引入外部知识但只擅长单跳查询，在多跳跨文档推理中表现不佳；现有图结构 RAG 或问题分解方法缺乏动态分解和有效过滤，导致效率与准确性较低。

Method: 提出 D2F-ReAG 范式：首先自适应当控推理深度——若根级推理可靠则直接生成答案；否则将问题逻辑分解为子问题，利用子问题验证后的推理结果来修正根级推理。

Result: 在三个多跳推理基准数据集上实验证明，该方法能有效处理复杂多跳问题。

Conclusion: D2F-ReAG 通过动态分解与过滤机制，显著提升了 RAG 在多跳推理场景下的表现，为复杂问答提供了一种更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often generate inaccurate answers due to their reliance on static internal knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) addresses this limitation by integrating external knowledge and excelling at single-hop queries. However, it struggles with multi-hop questions that require cross-document reasoning. Existing methods, such as graph structured RAG or question decomposition, often lack dynamic decomposition and effective filtering, which leads to lower efficiency and accuracy. To overcome these limitations, we propose Dynamic Decomposition and Filtering for Multi-Hop Reasoning-Augmented Generation (D2F-ReAG), a novel paradigm that adaptively controls reasoning depth by judging the reliability of the root-level reasoning. If the root reasoning is reliable, the model directly generates the answer. Otherwise, the question is logically decomposed into sub-questions, and the verified reasoning derived from these sub-questions is used to refine the root reasoning. Experiments on three multi-hop benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in handling complex multi-hop questions.

</details>


### [139] [The Evaluator Is Part of the Experiment: Measuring Open-Ended LLM Conformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04463)
*Alicia Guerra,Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的实验协议来测量LLM在开放任务中的从众行为。研究发现，仅以翻转率衡量是不足的，错误同伴信息会降低修订质量，且评估者（包括人类和GPT）并非中性，因此锚定校准至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM从众性的研究主要聚焦于可验证标签下的离散答案翻转。然而，开放式修订中答案质量是分级的、潜在的且评判不完美的，需要一种不同的测量策略来准确捕捉这种从众行为。

Method: 引入了一个实验协议，该协议在一个主同伴条件语料库和多个单独构建的分解语料库上实施。该协议能够分离出普通重答、候选内容暴露、捆绑的同伴呈现残差，以及评估者对可见同伴语境的方向性敏感性。实验使用了四个开放权重生成器和三个基准数据集。

Result: 在每一个生成器-数据集组合中，全错同伴输入都产生了最低质量的修订。盲评和知情评级对相同答案给出的评分不同：一位评估者向同伴认可的位置偏移，两位偏离，一位大致中立，GPT-4o和GPT-5.4-mini审计也呈现非中性。此外，锚定审计显示，简洁正确的锚点常常被误读，足以在未明确检查校准的情况下破坏潜在量表的稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于这些结果得出四个结论：翻转率不足以完全衡量开放式从众行为；错误的同伴信息会损害开放式修订的质量；评估者并非中性；锚定校准是必要的。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM conformity largely measures discrete answer flips under verifiable labels. Open-ended revisions require a different measurement strategy because answer quality is graded, latent, and judged imperfectly. We introduce an experimental protocol implemented across a pooled main peer-condition corpus and separately constructed decomposition corpora, allowing us to separate ordinary re-answering, candidate-content exposure, a bundled peer-presentation residual, and directional judge sensitivity to visible peer context. Across four open-weight generators and three benchmarks, all-wrong peer input produces the lowest-quality revisions in every generator-dataset cell. Blind and informed ratings of identical answers also differ by evaluator: one judge shifts toward the peer-endorsed position, two shift away, one is approximately neutral, and GPT-4o and GPT-5.4-mini audits are likewise non-neutral. Finally, an anchor audit shows that terse correct anchors can be misread often enough to destabilize the latent scale unless calibration is checked explicitly. These results support four conclusions: flip rates are insufficient as a complete measure of open-ended conformity, wrong peers harm open-ended revision, evaluators are not neutral, and anchor calibration is necessary.

</details>


### [140] [Energy- and Memory-Efficient PEFT Methods for Personalized On-Device SLMs on Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04488)
*Kuanysh Akhmetzhanov,Jurn-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种参数高效微调方法（全微调、LoRA、LoRA+、QLoRA、BitFit）在四种小型语言模型（TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen3-1.7B、Mamba-1.4B、Mamba-2-1.3B）上的表现，涵盖通用（GLUE）和个性化（LaMP）基准，并引入能量感知指标NetScore-E和内存感知指标NetScore-M。LoRA+在24个配置中19个取得最高NetScore-E，18个配置被选为最优方法；QLoRA在Transformer模型上可将峰值微调显存降低至LoRA的3.9倍，但在能量指标上仅一次被选中；TinyLlama-1.1B在能量和内存指标上均领先。结论表明紧凑SLM结合PEFT是实现节能个性化和设备端部署的实用路径，最优方法取决于主导约束：能量优先选LoRA+，内存优先选QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源受限设备上部署和个性化面临高VRAM、时间和能耗成本的挑战。参数高效微调（PEFT）小型语言模型（SLM）是一种有前景的替代方案，但现有研究缺乏跨架构、同时考虑通用和个性化基准并计入能耗的系统比较。

Method: 比较五种微调方法（全微调、LoRA、LoRA+、QLoRA、BitFit）在四个SLM（Transformer族：TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen3-1.7B；SSM族：Mamba-1.4B、Mamba-2-1.3B）上的表现，使用三个GLUE任务（SST-2、QNLI、STS-B）和三个LaMP个性化任务（LaMP-1、LaMP-2、LaMP-3）。采用能量聚焦的NetScore-E和内存聚焦的NetScore-M两个指标来反映设备端部署约束，并使用严格能量优先规则（最高NetScore-E，平局时用NetScore#）选择方法。

Result: LoRA+在24个配置中19个取得最高NetScore-E，13个取得最高NetScore-M，并在18个配置中被选为最优方法。QLoRA仅适用于Transformer模型，可将峰值微调显存降低至LoRA的3.9倍，因此在12个Transformer配置中5个取得最佳NetScore-M，但由于反量化开销，在能量优先规则下仅一个配置被选中。BitFit和全微调几乎没有竞争力。TinyLlama-1.1B在六个基准中五个引领NetScore-E，四个引领NetScore-M。

Conclusion: 紧凑型SLM搭配PEFT为节能个性化的设备端部署提供了实用路径。最优方法取决于主导约束：能量优先时选择LoRA+，内存优先时选择QLoRA。全微调和BitFit在两种约束下均不具备竞争力。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in large language models (LLMs), deploying and personalizing them on resource-constrained devices remains impractical due to high VRAM, time, and energy costs. Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) of Small Language Models (SLMs) offers a promising alternative, yet few studies compare PEFT methods across architectures using both general and personalization benchmarks while accounting for energy consumption. We compare five fine-tuning approaches (Full Fine-Tuning, LoRA, LoRA+, QLoRA, and BitFit) on four SLMs from two families (Transformer-based: TinyLlama-1.1B, Qwen3-1.7B; SSM-based: Mamba-1.4B, Mamba-2-1.3B) across three GLUE tasks (SST-2, QNLI, STS-B) and three LaMP personalization tasks (LaMP-1, LaMP-2, LaMP-3). Each configuration is evaluated with the energy-focused NetScore-E and the memory-focused NetScore-M, the two variants that reflect the constraints binding on-device deployment. Methods are selected with a strict energy-first rule (highest NetScore-E, ties broken by NetScore#). LoRA+ achieves the highest NetScore-E in 19 of 24 configurations and the highest NetScore-M in 13 of 24, and is the selected method in 18 of 24. QLoRA, available only for the Transformer models, cuts peak finetuning VRAM by up to 3.9x relative to LoRA and therefore takes the best NetScore-M in 5 of the 12 Transformer configurations, although its de-quantization overhead leaves it selected in only one of them once energy decides. BitFit and full fine-tuning are almost never competitive on either variant, and TinyLlama-1.1B leads the energy-focused NetScore-E on five of the six benchmarks and the memory-focused NetScore-M on four. These results show that compact SLMs paired with PEFT provide a practical, energy-aware path to personalized on-device deployment, with the optimal method set by the dominant constraint: LoRA+ for energy and QLoRA for memory.

</details>


### [141] [K-EXAONE 2.0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04505)
*Eunbi Choi,Kibong Choi,Sehyun Chun,Seokhee Hong,Junwon Hwang,Hyojin Jeon,Ahra Jo,Hyunjik Jo,Yeonsik Jo,Minhyeok Jung,Doyoung Kim,Heegyu Kim,Joonkee Kim,Seonghwan Kim,Soyeon Kim,Sunkyoung Kim,Yireun Kim,Yongil Kim,Byungoh Ko,Changhun Lee,Dohaeng Lee,Haeju Lee,Jinsik Lee,Kyungmin Lee,Minwoo Lee,Wonkee Lee,Sangha Park,Sungjune Park,Kwangrok Ryoo,Kijung Seo,Minju Seo,Yongwoo Song,Sejong Yang,Heuiyeen Yeen,Stanley Jungkyu Choi,Yemuk Choi,Yongchan Chun,Jiwon Ham,Dasol Hong,Sujeong Im,Kijeong Jeon,Gerrard Jeongwon Jo,Hyeongjun Jo,Yujin Jo,Jiyeon Jung,Naeun Kang,Daeseong Kim,Euisoon Kim,Hayeon Kim,Hyosang Kim,Myoungshin Kim,Unsol Kim,Youchul Kim,Chaeeun Lee,ChaeYoon Lee,Edward Hwayoung Lee,Honglak Lee,Hwansoo Lee,Minkyung Lee,Sangeun Lee,Solji Lim,Woohyung Lim,Chanwoo Moon,Jueun Mun,Jimin Park,Seojeong Park,Yongmin Park,Hyerin Seo,Donghyeon Shin,Donghyun Son,Eunyong Son,Kaehyun Um,Sihoon Yang,Chang En Yea,Sihyuk Yi,Kyungjae Yoo,Chansik Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: K-EXAONE 2.0 是 LG AI Research 开发的开源多语言基础模型，采用 MoE 架构，总参数量 750B，每个 token 激活约 37B 参数，支持最长 256K token 的上下文和 10 种语言。通过持续预训练、难度聚焦中训练和后训练，在智能体编码、长上下文理解、检索和安全方面显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是迈向全球前沿规模的基础模型，构建开放权重、多语言、安全且扎根于韩国社会文化语境的基础模型。

Method: 从 K-EXAONE 升级循环（upcycle），扩展架构为混合专家模型（MoE），总参数量 750B，每 token 激活约 37B。训练流程包括持续预训练、难度聚焦中训练（difficulty-focused mid-training）和后训练，以增强推理、智能体编码、多语言能力和安全性。支持最长 256K token 上下文，语言覆盖从 6 种扩展到 10 种。

Result: 在 9 个评估类别上全面超越 K-EXAONE，与开源模型保持竞争力。最大提升在智能体编码和长上下文理解，最突出优势在长上下文检索和安全方面。

Conclusion: 基于 Apache 2.0 许可证发布，使更广泛的 AI 生态能够评估、部署、适配和在此基础上构建。这标志着迈向全球前沿的起点而非终点。

Abstract: This technical report presents K-EXAONE 2.0, an open-weight multilingual foundation model developed by LG AI Research as a step in our effort toward global frontier-scale foundation models. Rather than training from scratch, we upcycle K-EXAONE and expand its architecture, yielding a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 750B total parameters and approximately 37B activated per token---more than three times the capacity of its predecessor. K-EXAONE 2.0 supports context lengths of up to 256K tokens and expands multilingual coverage from six to ten languages. Its training pipeline combines continual pre-training, difficulty-focused mid-training, and post-training to strengthen reasoning, agentic coding, multilingual capability, and safety grounded in Korean sociocultural contexts. Across nine evaluation categories selected to reflect the conditions of practical use, K-EXAONE 2.0 improves over K-EXAONE and remains competitive with open-weight models, showing its largest gains in agentic coding and long-context understanding and its clearest strengths in long-context retrieval and safety. Released under the Apache 2.0 license, K-EXAONE 2.0 enables the wider AI ecosystem to evaluate, deploy, adapt, and build upon it, while marking the beginning---rather than the endpoint---of our challenge toward the global frontier.

</details>


### [142] [RESPClinBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Clinical Decision-Making and Longitudinal Disease Management in Respiratory Specialty Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04514)
*Mouxiao Bian,Zhi Chen,Ruiyao Chen,Lu Lu,Hengrui Liang,Chaoyi Huang,Yiluo Lin,Jingru Ding,Yun Zhong,Yuming Su,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RESPClinBench是一个基于真实临床场景的呼吸科临床决策基准，包含427例COPD病例（AECOPD-PIM）和196例肺结节多模态病例（PNBIM），评估了7个当代大语言模型，最高平均得分71.22（Qwen3.6-27B），发现模型在影像幻觉（31.85%）、用药安全风险（26.93%）和严重医疗风险（8.16%）方面存在显著问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基准以考试为导向，无法代表呼吸专科诊疗所需的多模态解读、纵向风险评估、指南一致干预和全程管理能力，亟需构建贴近真实临床场景的评估基准。

Method: 从脱敏呼吸科临床数据改编案例，由3名主治医师修订案例、参考答案和原子临床行动点，1名高级专家交叉审核终裁；构建AECOPD-PIM（427例开放COPD案例）和PNBIM（196例结合CT与结构化信息的多模态肺结节案例）；7个模型通过标准化API（温度0，最大输出8192词元）生成4361条回复；自动评分框架计算原子行动召回率与基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge评估的算术平均分。

Result: 623个案例平均最终得分68.58；Qwen3.6-27B总分第一（71.22），Qwen3.5-397B-A17B在PNBIM领先（72.48），Qwen3.6-27B在AECOPD-PIM领先（71.11）；PNBIM中影像幻觉和严重医疗风险分别占31.85%和8.16%，AECOPD-PIM中用药安全风险和严重医疗风险分别占26.93%和1.44%。

Conclusion: RESPClinBench揭示了模型在多模态肺结节评估和纵向COPD管理中的任务特定局限；结合显式临床行动覆盖度、整体评估和独立安全标志为模型选择和前瞻性验证提供了临床依据。

Abstract: Background: Respiratory specialty care requires multimodal interpretation, longitudinal risk assessment, guideline-concordant intervention, and whole-course management, which are poorly represented by examination-oriented medical benchmarks. Objective: To develop RESPClinBench, a real-world scenario-based benchmark for respiratory clinical decision-making, and evaluate seven contemporary large language models across AECOPD-PIM and PNBIM. Methods: RESPClinBench cases were adapted from de-identified respiratory clinical data. Three attending-level respiratory physicians revised cases, reference answers, and atomic clinical-action points, while one senior respiratory specialist performed cross-review and final adjudication. AECOPD-PIM comprised 427 open-ended COPD cases, and PNBIM comprised 196 multimodal pulmonary nodule cases combining chest CT with structured clinical information. Seven models generated 4,361 responses through standardized API inference with temperature 0 and a maximum output length of 8192 tokens. An automated framework calculated the final score as the arithmetic mean of atomic-action recall and rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge assessment. Results: Across 623 cases, the mean final score was 68.58. Qwen3.6-27B ranked first overall at 71.22, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B led PNBIM at 72.48, and Qwen3.6-27B led AECOPD-PIM at 71.11. Imaging hallucination and serious medical risk occurred in 31.85% and 8.16% of PNBIM responses; medication-safety risk and serious medical risk occurred in 26.93% and 1.44% of AECOPD-PIM responses. Conclusions: RESPClinBench identifies task-specific limitations in multimodal pulmonary nodule assessment and longitudinal COPD management. Combining explicit clinical-action coverage, holistic evaluation, and independent safety flags provides a clinically grounded basis for model selection and prospective validation.

</details>


### [143] [ODRA: Synthesizing Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Sessions with Structured Chain-Of-Thought and Dynamic Patient Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04524)
*Javier Rodriguez-Juan,Hiba Arnaout,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez,David Tomás,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ODRA框架，通过基于CBT指南的思维链策略和抵抗编排器来合成治疗对话，有效解决现有方法中的患者谄媚问题，在治疗技能、CBT对齐和患者行为保真度方面显著优于现有方法，且基于该数据集微调的模型在应对合作与抵抗患者时表现出更优的治疗性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脚本方法和多智能体方法在合成CBT治疗对话时存在不足：脚本方法无法捕捉动态治疗互动，多智能体方法难以遵循CBT的顺序结构，且两者都面临谄媚问题（产生过度顺从的患者），无法真实反映临床场景。因此需要一种既能遵循CBT结构又能模拟真实患者抵抗行为的新框架。

Method: ODRA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于CBT基础指南（Beck, 2020）的思维链（CoT）策略，用于引导对话生成遵循治疗结构；(2) 抵抗编排器（Resistance Orchestrator），通过引导技术（steering techniques）生成与患者抵抗水平一致的行为，解决患者谄媚问题。

Result: 自动评估和专家评估显示ODRA在治疗技能、CBT对齐和患者行为保真度上显著优于现有方法；持证心理学家在13项临床指标中有12项偏好ODRA生成的对话。此外，在ODRA数据集上微调的模型在应对合作型和抵抗型患者时均表现出更优的治疗性能，证明显式抵抗建模能直接提升下游临床鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ODRA通过显式建模患者抵抗行为，成功生成了更真实、更符合CBT结构的治疗对话，并且这些合成数据可有效用于微调治疗模型，提升其在临床场景中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Synthetic generation of Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) sessions is challenged by two competing demands: adhering to strict therapeutic structure while modeling the resistant, unpredictable behavior of real patients. Existing script-based methods fail to capture dynamic therapeutic interactions, while multi-agent approaches struggle to adhere to CBT's sequential structure; both suffer from sycophancy, producing overly compliant patients that misrepresent real clinical settings. In this work we introduce ODRA, a novel framework for synthesizing therapy dialogues through a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) strategy grounded in foundational CBT guidelines (Beck, 2020). ODRA further incorporates a resistance orchestrator to solve patient sycophancy, which employs steering techniques to elicit behaviors aligned with their resistance level. Automated and expert evaluations show that ODRA significantly outperforms existing methods across therapeutic skills, CBT alignment, and patient behavioral fidelity, with licensed psychologists preferring ODRA sessions across 12 of 13 clinical metrics. Furthermore, models fine-tuned on our dataset demonstrate superior therapeutic performance against both cooperative and resistant patients, validating that explicit resistance modeling in synthetic training data directly translates to downstream clinical robustness.

</details>


### [144] [EuroExec: Frontier Language Models Fall Short of Expert Judgment on European Executive Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04549)
*Pau Arnal,Khaled Denfir,Danylo Smahliuk,Amrut Avhad,Marcus A. Castro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过4,000+小时人类专家评估，研究了六种前沿大语言模型在开放式复杂欧洲高管任务（EuroExec基准，413个真实案例问题）上的表现。最强模型仅解决56.9%的任务，而专家撰写的参考答案接近天花板，并在74%的直接排名中优于所有模型响应。研究表明，人类评估者在此类主观真实世界的开放性问题评估中不可或缺，自动评估方法存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM越来越多地用于解决开放式复杂问题，但现有评估方式对此类问题覆盖不足。作者希望系统评估前沿LLM在真实高管级别任务上的表现，并将其与专业人类标准进行比较，以了解当前生成式系统是否达到已被实际使用的专业工作水平。

Method: 构建EuroExec基准：413个开放式长文欧洲高管任务，由47位经过审核的领域专家基于真实案例经验编写。投入4,000+小时人类专家评估，采用多属性评分标准、逐项检查清单和偏好排序，提取聚合指标"解决率"。同时评估专家撰写的参考答案作为对照。通过严格的统计分析检查评估者一致性。

Result: 最强模型（性能最佳的LLM）仅解决56.9%的任务；专家参考答案接近天花板水平；在74%的直接排名中，专家答案优于所有模型响应。自动评估方法在此类问题上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在开放式专业高管任务上的表现远低于已被实际使用的专业工作标准。评估此类具有主观真实性的开放性问题，最有效的方法是使用经过严格一致性检验的人类评估者，自动测量方法在此场景下存在明显缺陷。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly put to use on open-ended complex questions, different in nature from the ones they are typically evaluated on. We dedicate more than 4,000 human expert hours to evaluate a selection of six frontier LLMs on a member of this class of problems: EuroExec, our introduced human expert-based benchmark composed of 413 open-ended long-form European executive tasks authored by 47 vetted domain experts, each question drawn from experience in a real case. Every response is manually evaluated through a multi-attribute rubric, an item-specific checklist of requirements, and a preference rank ordering, extracting an aggregate metric "Solve Rate". The strongest model solves only 56.9% of tasks, while expert-written reference answers judged blindly are solved at near-ceiling levels and are preferred over every model response in 74% of direct rankings, placing frontier generative systems well below the professional standard of work they are already used for. We see that the best way to extract this kind of conclusion is by employing human evaluators, carefully checking their consistency through rigorous statistical analysis, and observe that automatic measurements also fall short when evaluating on this case of real-world open-ended problems with a subjective ground truth.

</details>


### [145] [Relational Response Fields: A General Theory of Black-Box LLM Response Consistency and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04552)
*Song Zichen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出关系响应场（RRF）框架，通过将查询的多种变换下的响应表示为关系图，并定义γ_k(D,A)作为黑盒响应恢复的内在难度指标，从信息论角度分析了响应可恢复性的必要条件，证明了一致性不等于真实性，并给出了相应的稳定性边界和最优估计下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒语言模型可靠性方法（采样、提示、投票、验证、迭代修正）主要关注如何改进个体答案的质量，但缺乏对“响应集合本身是否可恢复”这一前提问题的理论理解。本文旨在回答：什么决定了黑盒响应集合的可恢复性？

Method: 将查询的多种变换（如释义、缩放、分解、重构等）下的响应建模为关系响应场（RRF），其中边传输编码有效响应在变换下应如何变化，锚点编码独立可信证据（如执行结果或验证器）。定义关系算子D和锚点算子A，对于最多k个被破坏的响应节点，提出γ_k(D,A)作为黑盒响应恢复的内在难度。该指标正值当且仅当每个k节点破坏可识别；给出确定性稳定性边界（与1/γ_k成正比）和匹配的两点极小极大下界。进一步区分信息论可识别性与凸优化所需的更强零空间条件，并推导稀疏场修复算法。

Result: 通过受控定理测试和黑盒数学/代码实验，评估了四个理论预测的后果：一致性-真实性分离、锚点相变、冗余饱和、以及跨模型跨任务的修复难度预测。结果表明γ_k(D,A)是可测量且与具体修复启发式无关的响应恢复实例属性。

Conclusion: γ_k(D,A)是衡量黑盒响应恢复难度的关键量，它揭示了关系一致性不等于真实性：仅依赖关系的方法对共享幻觉等零方向是盲目的。该工作为理解和设计可靠的响应恢复机制提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Black-box language-model reliability is commonly pursued by sampling, prompting, voting, verifying, or iteratively revising individual answers. We ask a prior question: \emph{what determines whether a collection of black-box responses is recoverable at all?} We represent responses to typed transformations of a query as a \emph{relational response field} (RRF). Edge transports encode how valid responses must change under paraphrase, scaling, decomposition, refactoring, or other task symmetries; anchors encode independently trusted evidence such as execution or a verifier. For relation operator $D$, anchor operator $A$, and at most $k$ corrupted response nodes, we identify $γ_k(D,A)$ as the intrinsic difficulty of black-box response recovery. It is positive exactly when every $k$-node corruption is identifiable; it gives a deterministic stability bound proportional to $1/γ_k$; and a matching two-point minimax lower bound shows that no estimator can improve this dependence. Thus consistency is not truth: relation-only methods are blind to null directions, including shared hallucinations. We derive sparse field-repair algorithms while separating information-theoretic identifiability from the stronger null-space conditions required by convex optimization. Controlled theorem tests and black-box mathematics/code experiments evaluate four theory-fixed consequences: consistency--truth separation, anchor phase transitions, redundancy saturation, and cross-model, cross-task prediction of repair difficulty. The results support $γ_k(D,A)$ as a measurable property of a response-recovery instance, rather than a score attached to one repair heuristic.

</details>


### [146] [Representing Visual Evidence for Item Difficulty Prediction: Visual Textualization and Image-Native Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04554)
*Han Chen,Ming Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Comparing textualization and image-native modeling for predicting item difficulty from visual content in mathematics items, finding both achieve similar performance but with complementary errors and different computational trade-offs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing approaches for predicting item difficulty from content textualize visual evidence, but the best way to represent visual information for difficulty prediction is unclear.

Method: Compare three interfaces: question text alone, visual textualization (expressing visual evidence in language), and image-native modeling (retaining original image). Train LLMs and VLMs on Eedi items with calibrated difficulty for regression.

Result: Both visual interfaces achieve lowest point estimates but cannot be reliably ordered. Open-VLM textualization gives lower RMSE for LLMs, while broader adaptation does for image-native VLMs. Test-time interventions show dependence on full-item image but cannot isolate visual component. The two visual interfaces make partially complementary errors and differ in computational workflow.

Conclusion: Textualization should not be treated as the only practical interface; image-native modeling is a competitive alternative whose effectiveness depends on VLM adaptation.

Abstract: Predicting item difficulty from content can provide an initial estimate for newly developed questions before sufficient student responses are available. Existing approaches typically represent the question stem and answer choices as text. When mathematics items contain visual components, a common pipeline first textualizes that evidence and then applies a text predictor. We ask: how should visual evidence be represented for item difficulty prediction? We compare question text alone, visual textualization, which expresses visual evidence in language, and image-native modeling, which retains the original image. Using Eedi items with difficulty calibrated from student responses, we train large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) directly for difficulty regression. Both visual interfaces achieve the lowest point estimates, although the leading systems cannot be reliably ordered. Open-VLM textualization yields lower RMSE point estimates for all evaluated LLMs, while broader adaptation does so for all image-native VLMs. Test-time interventions show dependence on the paired full-item image, but do not isolate the additional visual component. The two visual interfaces also make partially complementary item-level errors and differ substantially in computational workflow. Thus, textualization should not be treated as the only practical interface: image-native modeling is a competitive alternative whose effectiveness depends on how the VLM is adapted.

</details>


### [147] [STRIVE: Probing Reasoning Limits in Graded Plausibility Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04567)
*Bhiman Kumar Baghel,Anna Chrabaszcz,Tessa Warren,Michael Walsh Dickey,Haley C. Dresang,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRIVE是一个基于LLM的框架，用于自动生成和评估心理语言学研究中控制事件集（交叉合理性类别与难度级别），将原本劳动密集的手动构建过程规模化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理语言学研究需要构建精细控制的事件集来考察事件合理性知识如何支持语言处理。这类研究要求事件集在保持其他特征固定的同时，仅改变一个事件槽位来操纵合理性水平。手动构建这样的控制事件集十分劳动密集，迫切需要自动化的方法来减少人工投入。

Method: STRIVE框架以动词输入，构建共享事件框架，通过仅改变一个事件槽位同时固定其他所有槽位来生成每个条件的相应事件。采用全局推理草稿（scratchpad）和基于评估器的引导式精炼（evaluator-guided refinement）提升生成质量。在60个动词上对6个模型进行实验评估。

Result: 基线生成提示下GPT-5.1仅16.7%时间产生高质量事件集；加入全局推理草稿和评估器引导精炼后提升至75.0%。更强的推理努力提高了评估者与人类的一致性。但在合理性边界附近的事件仍最困难，人类分歧最大，最佳评估器在“不合理-困难”条件下仅达到57%准确率。

Conclusion: STRIVE为心理语言学研究提供了一种可扩展的方法，通过自动化初始事件集生成和评估来减少人工劳动。尽管在边界事件上仍需人工输入，但整体上大幅降低了手动构建事件集的工作量。

Abstract: Event knowledge concerns who does what to whom. Psycholinguists use event-plausibility judgments to examine how this knowledge supports human language processing. To isolate plausibility effects, these studies require controlled event sets in which one event slot varies across plausibility levels while all other event features remain fixed. Constructing such sets manually is labor-intensive. We therefore introduce STRIVE, an LLM-based framework for jointly generating and evaluating controlled event sets crossing plausibility class (plausible vs. implausible) with intended classification difficulty (easy vs. hard). Given a verb, STRIVE constructs a shared event frame, then produces one event per condition by varying one slot while holding all others fixed. In experiments with six models across 60 verbs, GPT-5.1 produced high-quality sets only 16.7% of the time using the baseline generation prompt. Adding a global reasoning scratchpad and evaluator-guided refinement raised this rate to 75.0%. Greater reasoning effort also improved evaluator--human agreement. Nevertheless, events near the plausibility boundary remain most difficult. They elicit the greatest human disagreement, and the best evaluator reaches only 57% accuracy on the implausible-hard condition, indicating a need for human input. Overall, STRIVE offers a scalable approach to reducing manual effort by automating initial event-set generation and evaluation for psycholinguistic studies.

</details>


### [148] [Relevant but Incomplete: Referential Dangling as a Paradigm-Level Failure Mode in Hard Prompt Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04569)
*Zhengpei Hu,Kai Li,Dapeng Fu,Xuechao Zou,Yuanhao Tang,Yue Li,Tengfei Cao,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 硬提示压缩（hard prompt compression）通过独立评分并保留高分单元来降低长上下文推理成本，但该方法存在结构性缺陷：独立选择会拆散依赖证据对，导致"指称悬空"（referential dangling）——即保留的文本包含答案，但被删除的文本定义了理解答案所需的实体。在0.30压缩比下，基于Qwen3-0.6B嵌入的压缩器Beaver在34-54%的桥接样本中使答案路径不完整。所有六种硬压缩器在HotpotQA上的悬空率高达60%，LongBench-v2单文档QA中每篇文档都包含至少一个悬空指称。重新插入缺失的支持段落（同时移除非支持段落以维持预算）可将准确率提升29-34个百分点（p<0.0001），恢复至少88%的差距。更强的答案模型无法吸收损失：GPT-5.5在压缩上下文上的准确率比保留两个支持段落的上下文低8.8点。他们训练了一个分类器来排序被省略的句子（判断是否需要用于解释保留文本），并在推理时重新插入高排名候选，在HotpotQA上使准确率提升4.7点，而压缩比仅从0.30变为0.31。硬压缩器应同时优化相关性和指称完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬提示压缩通过独立评分保留高分单元来降低长上下文推理成本，但独立选择可能拆散依赖证据对，导致"指称悬空"——即保留答案文本但删除定义该答案所指实体的关键上下文。现有压缩方法只关注相关性（relevance），忽视了指称完整性（referential completeness），导致压缩后的上下文虽然包含答案但无法正确解读。

Method: 1) 系统识别并命名"指称悬空"问题，定义其发生条件；2) 在多个多跳问答数据集（HotpotQA、MuSiQue、2WikiMultihopQA）上评估现有硬压缩器（包括Beaver、LLMLingua-2、Icter、Selective Context、Truncate、ICL）的悬空率；3) 通过受控实验量化悬空对准确率的影响：将缺失支持段落重新插入并移除同等预算的非支持段落；4) 测试更强模型（GPT-5.5）是否吸收损失；5) 训练紧凑型分类器，在推理时无需支持标注，通过排序被省略句子来识别需要恢复的指称补全文本，自动恢复。

Result: 1) 压缩比0.30时，Beaver在34-54%的桥接样本中留下不完整答案路径；2) 所有六种硬压缩器在HotpotQA上的悬空率高达60%；3) LongBench-v2单文档QA中每篇文档都包含悬空指称；4) 重新插入缺失支持段落（移除非支持段落维持预算）使Qwen3-8B准确率提升29-34个百分点（p<0.0001），恢复至少88%的差距；5) GPT-5.5在压缩上下文上比保留两个支持段落时准确率低8.8点；6) 自动恢复方法在HotpotQA上提升准确率4.7点，压缩比仅从0.30变为0.31。

Conclusion: 硬提示压缩器必须超越单纯的相关性优化，同时考虑指称完整性。独立选择策略会系统性破坏文本中的指代关系，导致压缩上下文虽然表面包含答案但实际无法解读。自动指称补全（在无需标注的情况下恢复被删除的关键解释性文本）是一个有效的改进方向。更强的模型无法自动补偿这种结构性信息缺失。

Abstract: Hard prompt compression reduces long-context inference cost by independently scoring tokens, sentences, or chunks and retaining the highest-scoring units under a budget. We identify a structural failure in this procedure: independent selection can split dependent evidence pairs, retaining one member while deleting the other. When retained text contains an answer but deleted text defines the entity needed to interpret it, we call the result referential dangling. At a compression ratio of 0.30, Beaver, which ranks coherent chunks using Qwen3-0.6B embeddings, leaves the answer path incomplete in 34-54% of bridge examples across three multi-hop question answering datasets. On a shared HotpotQA bridge set, all six hard compressors we test exhibit dangling at rates up to 60%, and every document in LongBench-v2 Single-Document QA contains at least one dangling reference. On dangling examples evaluated with Qwen3-8B, reinserting the missing supporting paragraph while removing nonsupporting paragraphs to maintain the token budget improves accuracy by 29-34 percentage points (p < 0.0001), recovering at least 88% of the gap to contexts retaining both supporting paragraphs. Stronger answer models do not absorb the loss: on MuSiQue, GPT-5.5 is 8.8 points less accurate on compressed contexts than on contexts retaining both supporting paragraphs. Finally, we train a compact classifier to rank omitted sentences by whether they are needed to interpret retained text and reinsert the top-ranked candidates without support annotations at inference. On HotpotQA with Qwen3-8B, this automatic restoration improves accuracy by 4.7 points while changing the compression ratio only from 0.30 to 0.31. Hard compressors should optimize both relevance and referential completeness.

</details>


### [149] [The Personalization Mirage: How LLMs Fabricate User Profiles, and Why Self-Monitoring Misleads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04570)
*Yushi Sun,Yanjie Zhang,Rui Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了个性化LLM中的过度推断（OI）现象，即模型编造超出证据支持的用户属性。作者构建了包含150个角色、6个任务和四层忠实度分类体系的MirageBench基准，在12个模型上评估了143,616条声明。发现所有模型均存在过度推断（平均41.6%），并揭示了自我监控反转现象：自我报告过度推断最少的模型反而被判定为编造最多。因此，外部验证比模型自我报告更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM已广泛部署，但其用户模型的忠实度尚未得到检验。过度推断会导致模型编造用户属性，可能产生误导或隐私风险，亟需系统评估。

Method: 提出MirageBench：包含150个角色（刻板印象、反刻板印象、中性各50个）、6个个性化任务（覆盖从开放式到确定性的“想象梯度”）、四类忠实度分类体系（由独立评判器操作化，与人类标注者验证：Cohen's kappa=0.863四类，0.900二类）。在7个模型家族的12个模型上评估了143,616条声明。

Result: 过度推断普遍存在：所有12个模型均有35%-49%的声明存在过度推断（均值41.6%）。发现自我监控反转：模型自我评估的OI与评判器测量的OI呈负秩相关（rho=-0.60, p=0.044）。OI具有任务依赖性（27%-59%），多轮对话中推断属性近似线性累积且少有修正。

Conclusion: 外部验证（而非模型自我报告）是可信个性化更可靠的基础。自我报告信心是误导性的模型比较信号，尽管单模型内部自审计仍有一定效度（AUROC 0.58-0.83）。

Abstract: Personalized LLMs with persistent memory are increasingly deployed, yet the faithfulness of their user models remains unexamined. We study over-inference (OI): the phenomenon where LLMs fabricate user attributes beyond what evidence supports. We introduce MirageBench, comprising 150 personas balanced across stereotypical, counter-stereotypical, and neutral profiles, 6 personalization tasks spanning an ``imagination gradient'', a four-way faithfulness taxonomy operationalized by an independent judge (validated against a blind human annotator on 400 claims: Cohen's kappa = 0.863 four-class, kappa = 0.900 binary), and a leaderboard of 12 models across 7 families on 143616 judged claims. We find that over-inference is pervasive: every one of the 12 models over-infers 35%--49% of its claims (cross-model mean 41.6%; claim-weighted 41.8%), with no model in this evaluation escaping it. Most strikingly, we surface a Self-Monitoring Inversion: at the model-selection level, models' self-assessed OI is negatively rank-correlated with their judge-measured OI (rho = -0.60, p = 0.044; exploratory, wide bootstrap CI [-0.90, +0.06], n = 12). The models that report the least over-inference tend to be flagged as fabricating the most, so self-reported confidence is a misleading signal for comparing models, even though within a single model self-audit still ranks that model's own claims moderately well (AUROC 0.58--0.83). We further show that OI is task-dependent (27%--59%) and that, in a multi-turn pilot, inferred attributes accumulate approximately linearly with little revision. MirageBench positions external verification, rather than model self-report, as a more reliable foundation for trustworthy personalization.

</details>


### [150] [When Memory Lies: An Empirical Study of Spatial Memory Staleness in VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04574)
*Yushi Sun,Yanjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了记忆增强的视觉语言模型（VLM）智能体在环境变化时，如何应对空间记忆陈旧（staleness）的问题。通过动态FrozenLake实验，发现文本可解性不等于视觉接地，信任陈旧记忆会带来安全风险，审计虽有助于减少错误但无法完全消除视觉接地不可靠时的差距。研究将空间记忆陈旧定性为一种安全故障模式，并指出可靠的视觉接地和记忆-观测冲突下的行动选择是核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强的VLM智能体依赖持久空间记忆，但环境变化会使记忆悄然过时，智能体可能无法察觉记忆与当前观测的矛盾，从而导致安全相关的错误。本文旨在探究智能体在面对自信记忆与矛盾观测时能否发现冲突，并评估当前模型的能力。

Method: 使用动态FrozenLake测试平台，设计陈旧性检测任务和下游导航任务。测试了三种闭源模型和三种开源VLM，在文本和图像输入模式下进行实验（共1,800次检测运行，12,000次文本模式导航，使用四个LLM导航器，共享50种子规模）。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）文本可解性不代表视觉接地能力，模型在文本中能标记陈旧条目，但在视觉F1得分上差异巨大（从0.887到0.067）；（2）信任陈旧记忆而不审计会带来安全风险，在GPT-4o设置中，信任原始记忆的智能体死亡次数是无记忆智能体的两倍以上；（3）审计有帮助但不能消除差距，透明读取时过滤器在文本模式下可降低安全成本，但视觉审计不可靠时过滤无一致收益。

Conclusion: 空间记忆陈旧是安全故障模式，可靠的视觉接地和记忆-观测冲突下的行动选择仍是记忆增强智能体面临的核心开放挑战。

Abstract: Memory-augmented VLM agents act on persistent spatial knowledge, yet that knowledge silently goes stale as the environment changes. We ask what happens when an agent must reconcile a confident memory claim with a contradicting observation, and whether current models can catch the conflict before it becomes a safety-relevant mistake. Using a dynamic FrozenLake testbed, we pair a staleness-detection task with a downstream navigation task across three closed-source models and three open-weight VLMs under both text and image inputs (1,800 detection runs, and 12,000 text-mode navigation episodes over four LLM navigators at a shared 50-seed scale). Three findings emerge. First, text solvability does not imply visual grounding: models that flag stale entries reliably from text nonetheless span vision F1 from 0.887 down to 0.067 on the identical grids, and the weakest keeps making fluent, confident decisions that ignore the image. Second, consuming stale memory without an audit is a safety liability: in our primary GPT-4o setting, an agent that trusts raw memory dies more than twice as often as the same agent given no memory at all. Third, auditing helps but does not close the gap: a transparent read-time filter removes much of the safety cost in text mode, yet even oracle stale labels bring no further significant gain on the current grid size, and when visual auditing is unreliable, filtering yields no consistent benefit. Together these results frame spatial-memory staleness as a safety failure mode and isolate reliable visual grounding and action selection under memory--observation conflict as the central open challenges for memory-augmented agents.

</details>


### [151] [Causal Evidence Extraction and Triangulation in Crisis Reports using Large Language Models: A ReliefWeb-based Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04576)
*Yuanjun Zhang,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段大语言模型（LLM）流水线，用于从人道主义报告中提取结构化的干预-结果证据，包含查询条件化提取和片段溯源；并设计了一种上下文保留三角测量方法，通过拉普拉斯平滑和 Level-of-Evidence 分数量化跨上下文收敛性。在专家标注的 100 份报告数据集上，闭源 LLM 最佳模型达到加权 F1 90.73%，Llama-3.1-8B 经监督微调后达到 94.15%。应用于现金援助时，与食物相关的结果呈现出强正向收敛（LoE=0.865）和稳定的长期轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义报告内容长、噪声多、主题混杂，难以整合与决策相关的因果证据。现有方法缺乏结构化提取和审计追踪，因此需要一种自动化流水线来提取干预-结果关系并评估证据的一致性。

Method: 两阶段 LLM 流水线：1) 查询条件化提取（Query-conditioned extraction）——将输出限制在指定的干预类别，减少检索导致的速度抽取；2) 片段溯源（Snippet grounding）——将每条关系链接到支撑文本，确保可审计性。此外，提出上下文保留三角测量（Context-preserving triangulation）：在“灾难×来源”单元格内聚合强度加权证据，应用拉普拉斯平滑，并等权重地合并单元格，计算 Level-of-Evidence (LoE) 分数来量化跨上下文收敛性。

Result: 在 100 份专家标注报告上，最佳闭源 LLM（GPT-4 等）加权 F1 为 90.73%，成本效益高；Llama-3.1-8B 经监督微调后加权 F1 达到 94.15%。在现金援助案例中，食物相关结果的 LoE=0.865，呈强正向收敛，且长期趋势稳定。

Conclusion: 所提流水线能有效从人道主义报告中提取结构化证据，三角测量方法可量化跨上下文证据收敛性，且模型性能优异。该方法具有可审计性和成本效率，适用于人道主义证据综合。

Abstract: Humanitarian reports are long, noisy, and multi-topic, making it difficult to consolidate decision-relevant causal evidence. We present a ReliefWeb study (2000-2024) and a two-stage Large Language Model (LLM) pipeline that extracts structured intervention-outcome records with direction and strength attributes. Query-conditioned extraction restricts output to a specified intervention class, reducing retrieval-induced over-extraction, while snippet grounding links each relation to supporting text for auditability and classification. In an expert-annotated dataset of 100 reports, the best closed-source LLM achieved a weighted F1 score of 90.73% with strong cost-efficiency, while Llama-3.1-8B with supervised fine-tuning reached 94.15% weighted F1 score. We further propose context-preserving triangulation that aggregates strength-weighted evidence within disaster$\times$source cells, applies Laplace smoothing and equally weights cells to quantify cross-context convergence via a Level-of-Evidence score. Applied to cash assistance, food-related outcomes show strong positive convergence (LoE=0.865) and stable long-horizon trajectories.

</details>


### [152] [Breaking the Curse ofMultilinguality inMany-to-Many Speech-to-Text Translation via a Resource-AwareMixture of Speech Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04586)
*Yexing Du,Kaiyuan Liu,Youcheng Pan,Bo Yang,Chengpeng Fu,Yu Wang,Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MSRT 提出了一种基于资源感知的混合语音编码器（MoSE）框架，通过显式语言路由器将每条语音分配给冻结的高资源专家编码器或可训练的低资源专家编码器，配合五阶段课程学习策略，仅需每语言10小时配对数据即可在45种语言的S2TT任务上实现SOTA，打破了多语言诅咒，同时提升高、中、低资源语言的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型在语音到文本翻译中面临多语言诅咒：单一共享语音编码器导致不同资源水平的语言竞争有限表示容量，高资源语言表现强但低资源语言性能严重下降。旨在解决该问题并提升多语言一致性。

Method: 提出MSRT框架，核心是资源感知的混合语音编码器（MoSE），包含一个冻结的高资源专家编码器（保留高资源能力）和一个可训练的低资源专家编码器（适应中低资源语言），通过显式语言路由器为每条语音分配专家。同时设计五阶段课程学习策略，显著降低数据依赖，每语言仅需10小时配对S2TT数据。

Result: 在45种语言上进行了全面评估，涵盖所有45×44个翻译方向。4B参数模型达到SOTA，超越更大基线的模型。MoSE同时提升高、中、低资源语言性能，低资源语言提升最大，打破了多语言诅咒且不损害高资源性能。

Conclusion: MSRT框架有效解决了多语言S2TT中的多语言诅咒问题，实现了多语言一致性的提升。代码和模型已开源，以支持未来多语言S2TT研究。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved significant success in speech-to-text translation (S2TT). However, when processing multilingual speech inputs, a single speech encoder shared across all languages suffers from the curse of multilinguality: languages at different resource levels compete for limited representation capacity, leading to strong high-resource performance but substantial degradation on low-resource speech. To address this problem and improve multilingual consistency, we propose MSRT, a novel framework built around a resource-aware Mixture of Speech Encoders (MoSE). MoSE uses an explicit language router to assign each utterance to an appropriate expert encoder. A frozen expert preserves high-resource language capabilities, while a trainable expert adapts to and specializes in medium- and low-resource languages. We further introduce a five-stage curriculum learning strategy that substantially reduces data dependence, requiring only 10 hours of paired S2TT data per language for effective alignment. We conduct extensive experiments on 45 languages, systematically evaluating all $45 \times 44$ translation directions. Our 4B-parameter model achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming substantially larger baselines. Empirical analyses show that MoSE improves high-, medium-, and low-resource languages simultaneously, with the largest gains on low-resource speech, thereby breaking the curse of multilinguality without compromising high-resource performance. To support future multilingual S2TT research, we release our code and models.

</details>


### [153] [EASy: Towards Efficient LLM-Based Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04588)
*Junnan Liu,Linhao Luo,Thuy-Trang Vu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EASy是一个可训练的智能体框架，通过强化学习联合优化任务性能与计算效率。它利用基于LLM的编排器，结合异构执行器的能力与成本画像，采用里程碑-规划-执行工作流分解复杂任务、构建依赖感知的执行图，并通过树状结构展开与多组件奖励进行训练，在数学推理、具身决策和深度研究基准上实现了比强基线更好的性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统主要优化任务成功率，对执行效率（如执行器能力限制和计算成本）考虑不足；基于路由的方法难以推理丰富的动态任务上下文、多步依赖关系和中间执行反馈，且对未见过的执行器泛化能力差。

Method: EASy包含三个核心设计：(1) 基于LLM的编排器，显式编码异构执行器的能力与成本画像，实现上下文敏感的协调；(2) 里程碑-规划-执行（Milestone-Plan-Act）工作流，将复杂任务分解为里程碑，构建依赖感知的执行图，分配执行器，并行独立步骤并依据中间结果调整后续决策；(3) 树状结构展开（tree-structured rollout）探索替代分解与执行计划，结合任务正确性、执行效率和轨迹完整性的多组件奖励进行强化学习训练。

Result: 在数学推理、具身决策和深度研究等多个基准上的大量实验表明，EASy在性能与效率的权衡上持续优于强智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: EASy通过显式建模执行器成本与能力、引入结构化工作流以及基于强化学习的联合优化，有效提升了智能体系统在复杂任务中的执行效率与任务性能，为构建更实用的多智能体协作框架提供了新方向。

Abstract: Agentic systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks by coordinating specialized LLM-based agents. However, most existing systems primarily optimize task success while giving limited consideration to execution efficiency under practical constraints such as executor capability and computational cost. Existing router-based methods have limited ability to reason over rich, evolving task contexts, multi-step dependencies, and intermediate execution feedback, and often generalize poorly to unseen executors. We propose EASy, a trainable agentic framework that jointly optimizes task performance and computational efficiency through reinforcement learning. EASy equips an LLM-based orchestrator with explicit knowledge of the capability and cost profiles of heterogeneous executors, enabling context-sensitive coordination beyond performance-only routing. It further introduces a milestone-plan-act workflow that decomposes complex tasks into manageable milestones, constructs dependency-aware execution graphs, assigns suitable executors, and parallelizes independent steps while adapting subsequent decisions to intermediate outcomes. To train the orchestrator, we develop a tree-structured rollout procedure that explores alternative milestone decompositions and execution plans, together with multi-component rewards that capture task correctness, execution efficiency, and trajectory completeness. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning, embodied decision-making, and deep research benchmarks show that EASy consistently achieves stronger performance-efficiency trade-offs than strong agentic baselines.

</details>


### [154] [When Absence Is Evidence: Evaluating Completeness-Sensitive Negative Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04591)
*Byoungjae Min,Kennedy Edemacu,Sae-Hong Cho,Yoonhyuk Choi,Beakcheol Jang,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs struggle with completeness-sensitive negative reasoning: they fail to reliably distinguish when a negative answer is justified (because evidence fully covers the query scope) versus when the answer should be unknown (because evidence only partially covers the query). The authors introduce CROWN-QA (CROWN-Synth + CROWN-Real) to study this, revealing an asymmetric failure mode where models treat implicitly partial evidence as if it were complete, and prompt interventions merely redistribute errors rather than resolve them.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs are frequently asked to verify absence (e.g., "Is X not in this document?"), but non-observation only justifies a negative answer when the evidence completely covers the query scope; otherwise, the correct answer is "unknown." Existing evaluations ignore this completeness-sensitive aspect, leading to unreliable and overconfident negative reasoning.

Method: The authors construct CROWN-QA, which includes (1) CROWN-Synth, a controlled synthetic dataset that fixes the question and observed facts while varying only query-relative coverage (complete vs. partial), and (2) CROWN-Real, a real-document contrast-set evaluation with controlled coverage variants. They test three LLM families using various prompts and structured certificate elicitation to trace error sources.

Result: Models exhibit unstable closure judgments and substantial over-closure, failing to reliably distinguish Certified-Negative from Unknown. The dominant failure is asymmetric: models often recognize implicitly complete evidence but treat implicitly partial evidence as query-covering. Prompting redistributes errors between over- and under-closure rather than resolving them. Structured certificate elicitation traces many errors to evidence-coverage mischaracterization. The partial-coverage asymmetry persists on real-document content, varying by model, prompt, and source.

Conclusion: Completeness-sensitive negative reasoning remains a persistent challenge for LLMs; current models cannot reliably determine when evidence coverage warrants a definitive negative answer versus abstention. Future work should focus on better coverage characterization and more principled handling of partial evidence.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often asked whether something is absent from a record, list, or retrieved context. Yet non-observation licenses a negative answer only when evidence completely covers the query scope; otherwise, the answer should remain unknown. We call this completeness-sensitive negative reasoning. We introduce CROWN-QA, comprising CROWN-Synth, a controlled paired core that fixes the question and observed facts while varying only query-relative coverage, and CROWN-Real, a real-document contrast-set evaluation with controlled coverage variants. Across three LLM families, models show unstable closure judgments and substantial over-closure, failing to reliably distinguish a justified negative answer (Certified-Negative) from insufficient evidence (Unknown). The dominant CROWN-Synth failure is asymmetric: models often recognize implicitly complete evidence yet treat implicitly partial evidence as query-covering. Prompting redistributes errors between over- and under-closure rather than consistently resolving them. Structured certificate elicitation traces many errors to evidence-coverage mischaracterization. CROWN-Real shows that the core partial-coverage asymmetry persists on real-document content, while its strength and the balance between over- and under-closure vary by model, prompt, and source.

</details>


### [155] [Evaluating Theory of Mind in Reasoning Models: Robustness over Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04646)
*Ian B. de Haan,Peter van der Putten,Max van Duijn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过改编机器心理学实验和标准基准测试，考察了推理型大语言模型在心理理论任务中的表现，发现其鲁棒性提升主要源于对提示变异和任务扰动的稳健性，而非获得了新的心理理论能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在心理理论测试中表现优异，引发对其能力的性质与有效性的争论；同时，基于可验证奖励的强化学习训练的推理模型在多项基准上取得显著进步，因此有必要探究这类推理模型在心理理论任务中的真实能力来源。

Method: 采用新颖改编的机器心理学实验以及已有的标准基准测试，对比分析推理型大语言模型在不同提示变异和任务扰动下的行为表现。

Result: 推理模型在心理理论任务中表现出对提示变异和任务扰动更强的鲁棒性，且这种提升至少部分源于模型在变异性条件下更稳定地得出正确答案。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持一种基于鲁棒性的解释，而非模型获得了新的、专门化的心理理论能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong performance on Theory of Mind (ToM) tests, prompting debate about the nature and validity of the underlying capabilities. At the same time, reasoning-oriented LLMs trained via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards have demonstrated notable improvements across a range of benchmarks. In this work, we examine the behavior of such reasoning models in ToM tasks using novel adaptations of machine psychological experiments together with results from established benchmarks. We observe that reasoning models consistently exhibit increased robustness to prompt variations and task perturbations. Our analysis suggests these gains come at least partly from models being more robust at reaching the correct answer under prompt and task variation. We read this as evidence for a robustness-based account rather than for a new ToM-specific ability.

</details>


### [156] [Easy to Complete, Hard to Choose: Investigating LLM Performance on the ProverbIT Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04670)
*Enrico Mensa,Lorenzo Zane,Calogero Jerik Scozzaro,Matteo Delsanto,Tommaso Milani,Daniele Paolo Radicioni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProverbIT基准，通过意大利语谚语任务评估13个前沿LLM（含推理模型），发现模型在谚语补全中表现良好，但在无正确答案的多项选择中性能急剧下降，推理模型也表现出对字面同义词的偏好和无法识别缺失选项，表明模型依赖记忆模式而非深层语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自然语言处理任务中表现出色，但对文化嵌入的语言表达（如谚语）的理解能力尚不明晰，现有研究缺乏针对意大利语谚语理解的系统评估。

Method: 构建包含100道多选题的ProverbIT基准，评估13个前沿模型（包括大型推理模型和传统LLM），设计三项任务：谚语补全、有正确答案的多选、无正确答案的多选，并对两个LRM进行思维链分析。

Result: 几乎所有模型在谚语补全任务中表现良好，但在无正确答案的多选格式中性能大幅下降；推理模型在推理过程中频繁提及正确谚语结尾却未能识别其缺失；模型强烈偏向选择字面同义词。

Conclusion: 当前LLM依赖记忆模式而非对文化表达式的深层语义理解，在比喻语言理解方面存在重要局限性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed computational linguistics and achieved remarkable performance across numerous natural language processing tasks, yet significant gaps persist in understanding how these systems process culturally embedded linguistic expressions. This paper introduces ProverbIT, a novel Italian benchmark comprising 100 multiple-choice questions designed to evaluate LLMs' ability to complete Italian proverbs. We assess 13 frontier models, including Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) and traditional LLMs, across three tasks: proverb completion, multiple-choice selection with correct answers, and multiple-choice selection without correct answers. Our evaluation reveals surprising results: while nearly all models demonstrate knowledge of the proverbs through successful completion tasks, performance drops dramatically when transitioning to multiple-choice formats without correct answers, with even state-of-the-art reasoning models showing substantial degradation. Through detailed Chain-of-Thought analysis of two LRMs, we uncover that models exhibit a strong bias toward selecting literal synonyms and frequently mention correct proverb endings during reasoning without successfully identifying their absence from the given options. These findings suggest that current LLMs rely heavily on memorized patterns rather than deeper semantic understanding of culturally grounded expressions, highlighting important limitations in their reasoning capabilities for figurative language comprehension.

</details>


### [157] [Kathleen Writes: Autoregressive Generation and Data Scaling Without Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04678)
*George Fountzoulas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文延续Kathleen系列，验证了基于波表编码器与多尺度混响状态的无注意力字节级架构在生成任务上的能力。结果表明：该模型在字节级语言建模上超越相同规模的Transformer；提出FORM DISTANCE度量以区分人类文本与机器生成文本；解码策略主导生成质量，检索增强解码可进一步降低距离，且增益严格依赖于来自训练语料的短语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无注意力架构（波表编码器+多尺度混响状态）在生成任务上的表现，而不仅仅是分类任务。

Method: 使用字节级语言模型（原始UTF-8），在WikiText-103上进行缩放实验；引入FORM DISTANCE度量（九种统计轴定义人类文本参考云，用于检测机器生成文本）；对比不同解码策略（如加宽采样器）以及检索增强解码（从训练语料中获取短语）；报告多种未奏效的架构改进。

Result: 缩放实验：0.5M参数的混响模型在2-512MB数据规模下均优于参数匹配的Transformer（512MB时1.84 vs 2.04 bits/byte）；FORM DISTANCE能显著区分人类文本与五种伪造文本；解码策略主导生成质量（加宽采样器使距离从3.17降至1.52）；检索增强解码进一步降至1.14；增益仅来自模型自身训练语料（40倍外部库无效）；计算词典达到学习表格94%的top-1准确率，参数仅1/5。

Conclusion: 无注意力架构同样具备生成能力，解码策略比架构本身更影响生成质量；检索增强解码有效但受限于训练语料；FORM DISTANCE可作为可靠的文本真实性度量；所有实验可在免费Kaggle T4上复现。

Abstract: Papers 1-2 of the Kathleen series showed that a byte-level, attention-free architecture built from a wavetable encoder and multi-scale reverberant state can match strong baselines on classification at ~450-700K parameters, without pretraining. We ask whether the same ingredients can generate. (1) Scaling: on byte-level language modeling (WikiText-103, raw UTF-8, no tokenizer), the reverberant model beats a parameter-matched transformer at every dataset scale measured (2-512 MB), e.g. 1.84 vs 2.04 bits/byte at 512 MB with ~0.5M parameters; the transformer needs more than 512 MB to match what the attention-free model learns from 32 MB. (2) Measurement: we introduce FORM DISTANCE, a non-parametric, gaming-resistant instrument for "reads like text": nine statistical axes of human text define a reference cloud, and five constructed fakes are all rejected. (3) Generation: decoding policy dominates architecture -- widening the sampler halves the same model's distance (3.17 to 1.52), and a retrieval-augmented decoding scheme takes the frozen model further (1.52 to 1.14) with no training step involved; the ablation attributes the gain to the sparse phrase dose itself, not the selection gate. The gain has a sharp boundary condition: the phrases must come from the model's own training corpus -- a 40x larger foreign library helps not at all, an effect the attention twin shares, consistent with in-context integration being a capability of scale. We also report four architectural additions that did not help, and a computed lexicon reaching 94% of a learned table's top-1 accuracy at one fifth of the parameters. Everything runs offline; all experiments are reproducible on a free Kaggle T4.

</details>


### [158] [IslamicTurathBench: A Multi-Task, Multi-Discipline Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on the Islamic Scholarly Tradition (turath)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04703)
*Shahd Gaben,Heba Sbahi,Samer Rashwani,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Mutaz Al-Khatib,Emad Mohamed,Somaya Eltanbouly,Mohammed Ghaly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍伊斯兰学术遗产基准数据集（IslamicTurathBench, ISTB），用于评估大语言模型在古典伊斯兰学术领域的多任务、多学科问答能力。该数据集包含3,465个问答对，涵盖35部权威著作、12个世纪的学术成果及7个核心学科，按学术需求（初、中、高级）和任务形式（选择题、段落理解、开放问答）组织，并提供了人类专家参考评分与10个系统的零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在宗教与文化领域（如伊斯兰研究）的应用日益广泛，但这些领域的核心概念、方法和争论缺乏高质量的标注资源。现有的伊斯兰学术传统（turath）需要专门的基准来评估LLM的表现，以推动该领域可靠的自然语言处理应用。

Method: 由领域专家开发并审校，从35部公认的经典著作中提取3,465个问答项，涵盖12个世纪、7个伊斯兰研究关键学科。数据集按两个维度组织：学术需求水平（初级、中级、高级）和任务格式（多项选择、段落理解、开放知识问答），并包含人类专家参考评分及10个系统的零样本基线。

Result: 构建了ISTB数据集，提供了人类专家参考评分和10个系统的零样本评估基线，支持跨文献、学科、学术需求水平和问题格式的可重复评估。

Conclusion: ISTB为评估LLM在古典伊斯兰学术领域的行为提供了标准化、可重复的基准，填补了该领域高质量标注资源的空白，有助于推动AI在宗教与文化传统中的可靠应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for question answering, education, and research, including in religious and cultural domains where answers depend on specialised source traditions. Yet in Islamic Studies, key concepts, methods, and debates preserved in the authoritative scholarly tradition, known as turath, lack high-quality annotated resources. We introduce IslamicTurathBench (ISTB), a multi-task, multi-discipline dataset for evaluating LLMs on classical Islamic scholarship. Developed and reviewed by domain experts, ISTB contains 3,465 question-answer items drawn from 35 recognised source works spanning more than 12 centuries of scholarship across seven key fields of Islamic Studies. To enable comprehensive profiling of model capabilities, ISTB is structured along two axes: scholarly demand (Beginner, Intermediate, and Advanced) and task format (multiple-choice questions, passage-based comprehension, and open-ended knowledge questions). ISTB includes aggregated scores from a scholarly human reference panel and zero-shot baselines from ten systems. The dataset supports reproducible evaluation of language-model behaviour across source works, disciplines, scholarly-demand levels, and question formats in a historically layered scholarly domain.

</details>


### [159] [EmpaAva: An Open-source Agentic 3D-Avatar Empathetic Live Chatbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04709)
*Jie Yang,Wenhao Xu,Shuhui Lin,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmpaAva 是首个开源、代理型 3D 虚拟形象共情聊天机器人，将共情响应生成从纯文本扩展到实时面对面交互，采用 LLM 协调的三代理架构，在情感理解、响应质量和视听一致性上超越多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将共情响应生成从纯文本交互扩展到实时面对面交互，并提供开源的、具有代理能力的 3D 虚拟形象解决方案，以填补现有工作在这一领域的空白。

Method: 提出 EmpaAva，其核心由一个 LLM 协调的三代理架构构成：感知代理、共情响应规划代理和具身渲染代理形成闭环；并配备响应规划层，将每个回复编译为可执行的多模态计划，确保语音、表情和渲染统一于共情意图。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中，EmpaAva 在情感理解、响应质量和视听一致性方面均优于纯文本、2D 说话人脸和多模态虚拟形象基线。

Conclusion: EmpaAva 是首个开源、代理型 3D 虚拟形象共情聊天机器人，作者已开源所有代码并提供了在线演示，证明了其在实时共情交互中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents EmpaAva, to our knowledge the first open-source, agentic 3D-avatar empathetic chatbot, which carries empathetic response generation (ERG) from text-only exchanges into live, face-to-face interaction. Through a video-call-like interface, a user speaks to a 3D digital human that reads their affect from speech and optional vision, and replies with emotional speech, lip-synced facial motion, and photorealistic 3D Gaussian rendering. At its core, an LLM coordinates a Tri-Agent Architecture, in which perception, empathetic response planning, and embodied rendering form a closed loop, paired with a Response Planning layer that compiles each reply into an executable multimodal plan, keeping voice, expression, and rendering on one empathetic intent. Building on strong open-source modules, EmpaAva supplies the intelligence that binds them into one controllable, inspectable experience. In automatic and human evaluations, EmpaAva surpasses text-only, 2D talking-face, and multimodal avatar baselines in emotion understanding, response quality, and audio-visual consistency. We open-source EmpaAva with an online live demo.

</details>


### [160] [Caching for the Future: Scrub Jay Episodic Memory Principles for Agent Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04746)
*Kartikey Singh Bhandari,Aarya Wadhwani,Dhruv Kumar,Pratik Narang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ScrubJay-MEM，一种受西部灌丛鸦情景记忆启发的 LLM 智能体外部记忆架构，通过类型条件化的时间衰减（per-memory type-conditioned temporal decay）将记忆编码为 What-Where-When 三元组并赋予可估计的易逝性系数 π_i 和效用时间域 τ_i，实现查询自适应检索和 O(1) 的回顾性更新。在提出的时间泛化测试（TGT）上取得正泛化差距（GenGap +0.108），在 MemoryAgentBench EventQA-64k 上 F1 比 Mem0 高 2.66、比 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 高 3.09，消融实验表明类型条件化衰减是必要组件，但在强 backbone 和事实巩固任务上优势缩小或反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体记忆架构将所有记忆视为同等持久，导致检索上下文被过时事实系统性污染；而不同内容类型的记忆有效性差异巨大，缺乏根据记忆类型进行差异化衰减的机制。

Method: 借鉴西部灌丛鸦的情景记忆特性，设计 ScrubJay-MEM 外部记忆存储：每个记忆编码为 What (内容)、Where (来源/上下文)、When (时间戳) 三元组，并自动分类估计易逝性系数 π_i 和效用时间域 τ_i；检索时根据查询进行自适应评分，更新时只需 O(1) 次 LLM 调用进行回顾性修正。同时构建时间泛化测试 (TGT) 基准和泛化差距 (GenGap) 指标。

Result: 在 TGT 上，ScrubJay-MEM 是唯一取得正 GenGap (+0.108) 的检索型系统；在 MemoryAgentBench EventQA-64k 上，使用 LLM backbone 时 F1 比 Mem0 提升 2.66，比 Qwen3-Embedding-4B 提升 3.09。消融实验去除衰减后 GenGap 下降 5.7 倍，证实类型条件化衰减的必要性。使用更强 backbone 时增益缩小，在事实巩固任务上效果反转。

Conclusion: 类型条件化的时间衰减是提升 LLM 智能体对易逝事实的时间推理能力的关键，但该贡献主要适用于时间推理任务而非事实巩固任务，且受益程度受 backbone 强度制约。

Abstract: LLM agents that persist across sessions accumulate stored memories whose validity varies enormously by content type, yet existing memory architectures treat all memories as equally persistent and systematically contaminate retrieved context with outdated facts. We show that per-memory, type-conditioned temporal decay, a property of western scrub jay episodic memory, can be operationalized as an auto-classified coefficient $π_i$ in an external LLM-agent memory store, yielding ScrubJay-MEM: each memory is encoded as a jointly-bound What--Where--When tuple with an estimated perishability $π_i$ and utility horizon $τ_i$, retrieved by query-adaptive scoring, and revised retroactively at $O(1)$ LLM calls per update. We introduce the Temporal Generalization Test (TGT), a benchmark with held-out retention intervals and a Generalization Gap (GenGap) metric. On TGT, ScrubJay-MEM is the only retrieval-based system with substantially positive GenGap ($+0.108$); on MemoryAgentBench EventQA-64k it improves F1 by $+2.66$ over Mem0 and $+3.09$ over Qwen3-Embedding-4B under a llm backbone. A decay ablation collapses GenGap by $5.7\times$, establishing type-conditioned decay as necessary for the result. Gains narrow under stronger backbones and reverse on fact-consolidation tasks, scoping the contribution to temporal reasoning over perishable facts.

</details>


### [161] [InsightEmb: Learning Action-Intent Embeddings for Agentic Insight Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04761)
*Tsz Ting Chung,Jiangnan Li,Jie Zhou,Mo Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出InsightEmb，一种对比嵌入框架，仅使用数学推理数据学习可迁移的、面向进展的检索几何结构，在无需环境特定训练的情况下，超越现有推理嵌入模型在动态智能体任务和静态技能检索基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
